(EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\\n\", \"total_chars\": 11287, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): trace SFT 效果因模型而异，\\\\\\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others\\\\\\\"\\\\n2. Ferrando et al. (2025): compact scratchpad ≥ full scratchpad\\\\n3. Gao et al. (2025): \\\\\\\"base model inductive bias outweighs SFT corpus\\\\\\\"\\\\n4. Meta CWM (2025): execution trace mid-training 需要 base model \\\\\\\"trace readiness\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Discussion 假说（论文 Discussion 中应呈现为 hypothesis，列出 alternative explanations）\\\\n- H1: Execution reasoning readiness（CRUXEval gap 预测 VOTA gap）— 最有文献支撑\\\\n- H2: Pretraining scale × structured data（Qwen 5.5T vs DeepSeek 2T tokens, 2.75x）— 合理但不能作为 definitive conclusion\\\\n- H3: Base model inductive bias ceiling（train loss 相当但泛化差距大）\\\\n\\\\n#### Alternative explanations（论文 Discussion 必须列出）\\\\n- Tokenizer 效率差异（不同 tokenizer 对 trace 文本的编码效率可能不同）\\\\n- Chat template 差异（不同模型的 chat template 对 trace 格式的适配程度不同）\\\\n- Instruction tuning 数据质量差异（RLHF/DPO 阶段的对齐方式可能影响 SFT 下游任务）\\\\n- ⚠️ 数据修正：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 预训练 **2T tokens**（87% code + 13% NL），来源 DeepSeek-Coder 技术报告。cross-model-trace-lit Worker 产出的 \\\\\\\"1.17T\\\\\\\" 和 \\\\\\\"4.7x\\\\\\\" 为错误数据，不得引用\\\\n\\\\n#### ⭐ 新视角：exception signal 免疫于 trace utilization gap [2026-05-15]\\\\n- VOTA 跨模型 gap 14.96pp → exception_only gap 仅 0.50pp\\\\n- 解释：exception events 结构简单（[ERROR] type: ... message: ...），不需要深度 trace reasoning 能力\\\\n- CRUXEval 差距只影响需要理解复杂 trace 结构（loop patterns, variable flow）的组件\\\\n- **论文可 claim**: exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic; trace complexity tolerance varies by model capability\\\\n\\\\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\\\\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\\\\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\\\\n- \\\\\\\"pass\\\\\\\"/\\\\\\\"fail\\\\\\\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\\\\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\\\\n\\\\n## 流程教训\\\\n- submit_training_job 必须用，不能用 nohup\\\\n- Worker 汇报需要交叉验证（exit code + log）\\\\n- [2026-05-15] 数据格式化 token budget 必须扣除 chat template overhead\\\\n- [2026-05-15] 所有训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing\\\\n- [2026-05-15] HuggingFace 下载设 HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1，优先 ModelScope CDN\\\\n- [2026-05-15] Worker 长时间无产出必须主动 get_worker_status(verbose=True) 排查\\\\n- [2026-05-15] bash -c 内部必须显式 export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={N}\\\\n- [2026-05-15] GPU 碰撞：提交前 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 无其他进程\\\\n- [2026-05-15] **同 GPU 并行 eval+training 严重降速**：cuda:1 上 eval+training 并行导致训练从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step。eval 必须用独立 GPU 或等训练完成。不要因\\\\\\\"eval 几分钟就完\\\\\\\"而在同一 GPU 上并行\\\\n- [2026-05-15] **重复 Worker 竞态**：两个同名 Worker 相隔 30 秒启动，后者看到前者的修改误判为\\\\\\\"原有\\\\\\\"。派 Worker 前查 list_workers 确认无重复任务\\\\n- [2026-05-15] **Bootstrap 方法一致性**：同一论文表格中所有 pairwise CI 必须用同一方法（paired 或 unpaired）。evaluate.py 必须加 --save_predictions 保存 per-sample 结果\\\\n\\\\n## 文献定位 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\\\\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\\\\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\\\\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\\\\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\\\\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\\\\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 11287, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

seed42 完全一致 ✅\\n  - seed123: 91.09% — **离群值**（LoRA 初始化 bad luck）\\n- **2/3 seeds 鲁棒 (99.33%)**，Claim 1 可保留，加 LoRA sensitivity limitation\\n- Qwen 3 seeds: 99.83% ± 0.00（完全稳定）\\n- 跨模型 gap: Qwen 99.83% vs DeepSeek 99.33% = **-0.50pp**（robust）\\n- seed123 离群值特征: train_loss=0.122 偏高, fail_recall=0.705, epoch1 token accuracy=92.55%（从一开始就没学好）\\n- **论文措辞**: exception signal 跨模型有效 (99.33% on DeepSeek)，但 LoRA 初始化偶尔导致收敛到差的局部最优 (1/3 seeds)\\n- Director 决策：exception_only seed robustness 优先于 VOTA seed robustness\\n  - **Framing 备选 B（epoch selection）已排除**，只剩 A（model-dependent ceiling）或 C（LoRA sensitivity on weaker models）\\n\\n#### DeepSeek VOTA 详细数据\\n- **DeepSeek VOTA accuracy: 84.45%** [82.6%, 86.4%]，使用 **epoch 3 final** checkpoint\\n- **DeepSeek no_trace accuracy: 73.36%** [70.8%, 75.7%]，lift 仅 45.4%\\n- **DeepSeek exception_only: 99.33%** — within-model lift +25.97pp over no_trace\\n\\n#### ⚠️ Bootstrap 方法一致性待修（非紧急）[2026-05-15]\\n- Qwen 实验用 **paired** bootstrap（有 per-sample predictions）\\n- DeepSeek 当前用 **unpaired** bootstrap（缺 per-sample predictions）→ CI 更宽，是保守估计\\n- **论文同一张表不能混用两种方法**\\n- **待办**: 为 deepseek_vota_seed42 和 deepseek_no_trace_seed42 重跑 `evaluate.py --save_predictions`，再用 paired bootstrap 重算 CI\\n- 当前 unpaired 结果 (+11.09pp [+7.90, +14.37]) 方向正确，paired CI 只会更窄\\n\\n#### eval_loss 与 verdict accuracy 解耦（关键发现）[2026-05-15]\\n- Epoch 1: eval_loss 0.068, accuracy **78.15%**, fail_recall **23.3%**\\n- Epoch 3: eval_loss 0.112, accuracy **84.45%**, fail_recall **45.4%**\\n- eval_loss 上升 (+65%) 但 accuracy 提升 (+6.3pp), fail_recall 大幅提升 (+22.1pp)\\n- **结论**：eval_loss（token-level calibration）与 binary classification accuracy 解耦。eval_loss 不是 verdict task 的可靠 checkpoint selection criterion\\n- **论文价值**: Discussion 中应讨论此现象\\n\\n### `load_best_model_at_end` 代码版本时间线 [2026-05-15]\\n- **13:24 之前**的所有实验：使用 epoch 3 final checkpoint（包括全部 Qwen + deepseek_vota/no_trace/exception_only）\\n- **13:24 Worker 新增** L149-152：load_best_model_at_end=True 等 4 行\\n- **deepseek_full_trace 必须用旧代码**（已注释为 # TEMP_DISABLED），保持 DeepSeek 组内一致\\n- **Qwen seed 实验**可用新代码（Qwen 组内一致即可）\\n\\n### 关键结论修正 [2026-05-15]\\n- v2 full_trace 28.4% 根因是 chat template overhead bug，v5 修复后 81.8%\\n\\n### 决策教训 [2026-05-15]\\n- 判断 data leakage 必须看原始数据内容，不能仅凭比率+accuracy 异常推断\\n\\n## 数据集重复分析 [2026-05-15]\\n\\n### 重复率统计\\n| 文件 | 总行数 | 去重后 | 重复率 |\\n|------|--------|--------|--------|\\n| exception_only_train.jsonl | 4720 | 2858 | 39.45% |\\n| exception_only_test.jsonl | 1190 | 607 | 48.99% |\\n| raw_traces.jsonl | 5910 | 5803 | 1.81% |\\n\\n### 重复原因\\nexception_only 高重复率源于同一题目的多个 passing 解在 exception_only 格式下产生相同的 (input, output)——通过的解无异常事件，trace 内容相同，10 个候选中多个正确解被映射为同一条训练样本。\\n\\n### Train-test 泄漏检查\\n**Train-test input overlap: 0（无泄漏）** ✅\\n\\n### 待补充 Limitation\\n论文 Limitations 缺少 pass/fail 分布依赖 LLM 能力的讨论。建议补充文本（接现有 L49 \\\"for transparency.\\\" 之后）：\\n> \\\"Moreover, the pass/fail distribution in the dataset is determined by the generating model's coding ability; a stronger or weaker generator would yield a different class balance, potentially affecting task difficulty and the generalizability of our accuracy estimates.\\\"\\n\\n## DeepSeek vs Qwen trace utilization gap — 文献解释（HYPOTHESIS，非 definitive）[2026-05-15]\\n\\n### CRUXEval 执行推理能力差距\\n| Model | CRUXEval-I (CoT) | CRUXEval-O (CoT) |\\n|-------|-------------------|-------------------|\\n| Qwen2.5-Coder-7B | 56.5% | 56.0% |\\n| DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | 39.0% | 41.0% |\\n| **差距** | **+17.5pp** | **+15.0pp** |\\n\\nCRUXEval 15-17pp 差距与 VOTA accuracy 14.75pp 差距吻合。\\n\\n### 关键文献\\n1. Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): trace SFT 效果因模型而异，\\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others\\\"\\n2. Ferrando et al. (2025): compact scratchpad ≥ full scratchpad\\n3. Gao et al. (2025): \\\"base model inductive bias outweighs SFT corpus\\\"\\n4. Meta CWM (2025): execution trace mid-training 需要 base model \\\"trace readiness\\\"\\n\\n### Discussion 假说（论文 Discussion 中应呈现为 hypothesis，列出 alternative explanations）\\n- H1: Execution reasoning readiness（CRUXEval gap 预测 VOTA gap）— 最有文献支撑\\n- H2: Pretraining scale × structured data（Qwen 5.5T vs DeepSeek 2T tokens, 2.75x）— 合理但不能作为 definitive conclusion\\n- H3: Base model inductive bias ceiling（train loss 相当但泛化差距大）\\n\\n#### Alternative explanations（论文 Discussion 必须列出）\\n- Tokenizer 效率差异（不同 tokenizer 对 trace 文本的编码效率可能不同）\\n- Chat template 差异（不同模型的 chat template 对 trace 格式的适配程度不同）\\n- Instruction tuning 数据质量差异（RLHF/DPO 阶段的对齐方式可能影响 SFT 下游任务）\\n- ⚠️ 数据修正：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 预训练 **2T tokens**（87% code + 13% NL），来源 DeepSeek-Coder 技术报告。cross-model-trace-lit Worker 产出的 \\\"1.17T\\\" 和 \\\"4.7x\\\" 为错误数据，不得引用\\n\\n#### ⭐ 新视角：exception signal 免疫于 trace utilization gap [2026-05-15]\\n- VOTA 跨模型 gap 14.96pp → exception_only gap 仅 0.50pp\\n- 解释：exception events 结构简单（[ERROR] type: ... message: ...），不需要深度 trace reasoning 能力\\n- CRUXEval 差距只影响需要理解复杂 trace 结构（loop patterns, variable flow）的组件\\n- **论文可 claim**: exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic; trace complexity tolerance varies by model capability\\n\\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\\n- \\\"pass\\\"/\\\"fail\\\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\\n\\n## 流程教训\\n- submit_training_job 必须用，不能用 nohup\\n- Worker 汇报需要交叉验证（exit code + log）\\n- [2026-05-15] 数据格式化 token budget 必须扣除 chat template overhead\\n- [2026-05-15] 所有训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing\\n- [2026-05-15] HuggingFace 下载设 HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1，优先 ModelScope CDN\\n- [2026-05-15] Worker 长时间无产出必须主动 get_worker_status(verbose=True) 排查\\n- [2026-05-15] bash -c 内部必须显式 export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={N}\\n- [2026-05-15] GPU 碰撞：提交前 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 无其他进程\\n- [2026-05-15] **同 GPU 并行 eval+training 严重降速**：cuda:1 上 eval+training 并行导致训练从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step。eval 必须用独立 GPU 或等训练完成。不要因\\\"eval 几分钟就完\\\"而在同一 GPU 上并行\\n- [2026-05-15] **重复 Worker 竞态**：两个同名 Worker 相隔 30 秒启动，后者看到前者的修改误判为\\\"原有\\\"。派 Worker 前查 list_workers 确认无重复任务\\n- [2026-05-15] **Bootstrap 方法一致性**：同一论文表格中所有 pairwise CI 必须用同一方法（paired 或 unpaired）。evaluate.py 必须加 --save_predictions 保存 per-sample 结果\\n\\n## 文献定位 [2026-05-15]\\n- Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\\n\", \"total_chars\": 11287, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — trace-verdict-study\\n\\n> v2.9 — [UPDATED 2026-05-15] Claim 1 FINALIZED (D029): DeepSeek 3-seed 完整数据 — seed42=99.33%, seed456=99.33%, seed123=91.09%。2/3 seeds 鲁棒，seed123 为 LoRA 初始化离群值。Claim 1 定稿为 \\\"consistently best component, seed-dependent on DeepSeek\\\"。新增 D10: train_loss≈但 accuracy 差 8pp（token-level loss 掩盖 sample-level 差异）。数据集来源：HumanEval (164) + MBPP-sanitized (427) = 591 problems × 10 LLM-generated solutions = 5910 samples。\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR submission)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nVerdict prediction (pass/fail) is fundamentally a **fault detection** task. The most effective signal is the **exception event** — a compact, structured diagnostic moment that consistently outperforms all other trace representations across models (Qwen 99.83% ± 0.00 across 3 seeds; DeepSeek 91-99% depending on seed, but always > VOTA 84.45% and no_trace 73.36%). In contrast, richer trace representations that require deeper execution reasoning show model-dependent effectiveness: VOTA gap = 14.96pp, no_trace gap = 1.77pp. Information density — not training signal distribution — is the primary bottleneck: even oracle loss masking (label-only) cannot close the 13pp gap between full traces and targeted extraction. The design principle: **extract the highest-specificity diagnostic signal; discard everything else.** Cross-model absolute performance depends on LoRA initialization stability, but exception_only's relative dominance over other components is robust.\\n\\nVOTA's primary contribution is **fault indicator isolation** — automatically extracting exception events from noisy full traces — rather than generic trace compression. The \\\"compression helps\\\" narrative was a stepping stone; the real mechanism is diagnostic signal extraction.\\n\\n## Core Claims (3)\\n\\n### Claim 1: Exception events are the most effective trace component for verdict prediction\\n\\nException summaries consistently achieve the highest verdict prediction accuracy across both models tested, outperforming all other trace representations in every seed. On Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B, performance is perfectly robust: 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds). On DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B, absolute accuracy is seed-dependent (seed42=99.83% epoch1, seed123=91.43% epoch1; Δ=8.40pp), consistent with LoRA initialization sensitivity — but exception_only still dominates VOTA (84.45%) and no_trace (73.36%) in all seeds. This contrasts with VOTA (cross-model gap = 14.96pp) and no_trace (gap = 1.77pp). Exception events are structurally simple (`[ERROR] type: ... message: ...`) and do not require deep trace reasoning.\\n\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: Qwen 99.83%, ECE=0.0027\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: DeepSeek 99.83% (epoch1) / 99.33% (epoch3)\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed456`: DeepSeek 99.33% (epoch3), CI [98.82, 99.75] — 与 seed42 完全一致\\n-
